## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「信頼性を重視し、届いたかを確認しながら順番どおりに送る」方式、UDPは「確認を省いて速く送る」方式です。

Web閲覧やメールなど欠けると困る通信はTCP、動画や音声の配信、オンラインゲームなど多少の欠落より遅延を避けたい通信はUDPが使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para ordenar tu rama local antes de compartirla, porque deja el historial lineal. Usa merge para integrar una rama que ya es pública o compartida, porque no reescribe commits y no rompe a quien ya tiene esa historia.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、保证可靠有序地送达；UDP 不建连接、不保证送达，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and unpushed, to tidy up its commits and keep history linear before you share it. Merge when others already have the branch or you've pushed it, since rebasing rewrites history they depend on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径就行，直接把它跟在 `bun test` 后面：

```sh
bun test tests/user.test.ts
```

不想写全路径，可以只给一段文件名，Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符串的测试文件：

```sh
bun test user
```

如果只想跑某一个测试用例，用 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按名字过滤：

```sh
bun test tests/user.test.ts -t "登录成功"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to the data is written down first, before it is applied to the database itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart to recover the changes that never made it to the main data files.
```

--- step 1: user says: 我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快
  [partner ack line] 好，我先找上个月在 haswell 上做的那个 Rust 构建，找到就接着做增量构建。
  [sent to task rust_haswell] 用户说："我上个月在 haswell 上那个 Rust 构建的，再接着把增量构建也弄快"。

接着做：增量构建也要弄快。冷构建已经从 14 分钟降到 6 分钟（sccache + mold），这次只看增量构建。

完成标准：在 haswell 上测量改动一个文件后的增量构建耗时，改前改后各给出数字；找到并处理拖慢增量构建的原因（比如 incremental 设置、codegen-units、缓存命中、链接阶段等），把改动提交到当前分支，并附上前后对比的命令输出。

不在范围内：不合并到主分支，不推送，不开 PR；不改动已经达成的冷构建配置，除非它直接导致增量变慢。

地点：haswell 上的 hawdb-rs 仓库，沿用原来的分支。
  [retitled task rust_haswell] haswell 上 Rust 构建提速（冷构建 + 增量构建）
  [also used tools] task_find
```

### board-offer-go-en

```
(earlier) user: Run hawdb's write benchmark every night and tell me how it went.
(earlier) partner ack: On it: every night at 23:00.  → task "Nightly hawdb write benchmark" working; its report: Night 3: 182k writes/s, p99 4.1 ms, level with last night (181k, 4.0 ms). Compaction stalls: 0.
(earlier) partner: Night 3: 182k writes/s at p99 4.1 ms, level with last night; no compaction stalls.
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "bench"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done. The board has the nightly write benchmark results. Night 3 is at 182k writes/s with p99 4.1 ms and no compaction stalls. Its card is below.
  [an app card under the message: made "hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2018 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="latest-sub">writes/s</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty">Nothing yet. Each night's result will land here.</div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  function fmt(n) { return n == null ? "—" : n.toLocaleString("en-US"); }
  function render(nights) {
    var list = document.getElementById("list");
    var empty = document.getElementById("empty");
    list.textContent = "";
    var rows = Array.isArray(nights) ? nights : [];
    empty.style.display = rows.length ? "none" : "block";
    rows.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row";
      var main = document.createElement("div");
      main.className = "k-row-main";
      main.textContent = r.night + (r.date ? " · " + r.date : "");
      var sub = document.createElement("div");
      sub.className = "k-row-sub";
      sub.textContent = "p99 " + (r.p99 == null ? "—" : r.p99 + " ms") +
        " · compaction stalls " + (r.stalls == null ? "—" : r.stalls);
      var val = document.createElement("div");
      val.className = "k-row-value";
      val.textContent = r.writes == null ? "—" : Math.round(r.writes / 1000) + "k/s";
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(val);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    var latest = rows[rows.length - 1];
    document.getElementById("latest").textContent = latest ? Math.round(latest.writes / 1000) + "k/s" : "—";
    document.getElementById("latest-sub").textContent = latest
      ? latest.night + " · p99 " + latest.p99 + " ms · " + fmt(latest.stalls) + " compaction stalls"
      : "writes/s";
  }
  render(ducter.get("nights"));
  ducter.onChange(function () { render(ducter.get("nights")); });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"nights":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":181000,"p99":4,"stalls":null},{"night":"Night 3","date":"Nov 22","writes":182000,"p99":4.1,"stalls":0}]}

### app-make-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 给我做个小应用，记一下这趟北京出差的花销，按天列，带个合计
  [partner message] 做好了，记一笔就自动按天加总。
  [an app card under the message: made "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3805 characters): <style>
.k-chips{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin:8px 0}
.k-field{margin:10px 0}
.k-field label{display:block;font-size:13px;color:var(--d-muted);margin-bottom:4px}
.k-input,.k-num{width:100%;box-sizing:border-box;padding:10px;border:1px solid var(--d-line);border-radius:10px;background:var(--d-sunken);color:var(--d-ink);font-size:16px}
.k-btn{width:100%;padding:12px;border-radius:10px;border:1px solid var(--d-line);background:var(--d-card);color:var(--d-ink);font-size:16px;margin-top:6px}
.k-btn.primary{background:var(--d-accent);color:#fff;border-color:transparent}
.k-day{margin-top:14px}
.k-day-total{float:right;color:var(--d-muted)}
.k-row{cursor:pointer}
</style>
<main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
  </div>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-h">记一笔</div>
    <div class="k-chips" id="days"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="what">花在什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="比如 午饭、打车"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="amt">多少钱</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder= … >`;
      }).join('')
    : '<div class="k-empty">还没有花销。选好第几天，填上花在哪、多少钱，点「记上」。</div>';
  ducter.summary(exp.length ? `${exp.length} 笔,合计 ${money(total)}` : '还没记');
}

function load() {
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('exp')).then(v => { exp = Array.isArray(v) ? v : []; render(); });
}

$('days').addEventListener('click', e => {
  const b = e.target.closest('button[data-d]');
  if (!b) return;
  cur = Number(b.dataset.d);
  render();
});

$('add').addEventListener('click', () => {
  const amount = parseFloat($('amt').value);
  if (!(amount > 0)) { $('amt').focus(); return; }
  const what = $('what').value.trim() || '花费';
  exp.push({ day: cur, what, amount: Math.round(amount * 100) / 100 });
  ducter.set('exp', exp);
  $('what').value = '';
  $('amt').value = '';
  render();
});

$('list').addEventListener('click', async e => {
  const row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
  if (!row) return;
  const e0 = exp[Number(row.dataset.i)];
  if (!e0) return;
  const a = await ducter.ask(`删掉「${e0.what} ${money(e0.amount)}」？`);
  if (a && a !== 'no' && a !== false) {
    exp.splice(Number(row.dataset.i), 1);
    ducter.set('exp', exp);
    render();
  }
});

ducter.onChange(load);
load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

<main class="k-app">

<div class="k-sum-label">Today</div>
<div class="k-sum-value" id="today">0 of … ,h.id),s=streak(h.id),b=best(h.id);
    return '<button class="hb-row" data-h="'+h.id+'">'+

      '<span class="hb-check'+(on?' on':'')+'">'+(on?'✓':'')+'</span></button>';
  }).join('');
  document.querySelectorAll('.hb-row').forEach(btn=>btn.onclick=()=>toggle(btn.dataset.h));

  const cells=[];
  for(let i=13;i>=0;i--)cells.push(addDays(now,-i));
  document.getElementById('grid').innerHTML=HABITS.map(h=>
    '<div class="hb-grid"><div class="hb-label">'+h.name+'</div><div class="hb-cells">'+
    cells.map(d=>{
      const kk=keyOf(d);
      return '<span class="hb-cell'+(isDone(kk,h.id)?' on':'')+(kk===k?' today':'')+'" title="'+kk+'"></span>';

```
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 加个合计，放在最上面，大一点

  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <ul class="k-list" id="list"></ul>
  </div>
  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>什么</label><input class="k-input" id="what"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-20"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN",{maximumFractionDigits:2});}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+Number(x.yuan||0),0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=fmt(total);
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?("共 "+xs.length+" 笔"):"还没记";
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");
  ul.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const li=document.createElement("li");
    li.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("span");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("span");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("span");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    li.append(main,sub,val);
    ul.appendChild(li);
  }
  if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="这里会列出每一笔花销";ul.replaceWith(e);}
}
document.getElementById("add").addEventListener("click",()=>{
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(!what||isNaN(yuan))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what,yuan,day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
});
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
ducter.summary(fmt(1966)+" · 3 笔");
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

function toggleEdit(id){

function saveEdit(id){
  var t = document.getElementById('e-t').value.trim();
  var m = document.getElementById('e-ml').value.trim();
  if (!validTime(t) || !validMl(m)) return;
  feeds = feeds.map(function(f){
    return f.id === id ? { id: f.id, date: f.date, time: t, ml: Number(m) } : f;
  });
  editing = null;
  save();
  render();
}

function removeFeed(id){
  feeds = feeds.filter(function(f){ return f.id !== id; });
  editing = null;
  save();
  render();
}

ducter.onChange(load);

### picture-question-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 图里这是什么？适合放在空状态里吗？ (with a picture: dev_laptop_awake.webp)
  [partner message] 这是一个 3D 风格的卡通笔记本电脑插画：奶油色的机身和键盘，深藏青色的屏幕边框，屏幕是空白的。

适合放在空状态里。空白屏幕正好对应"这里还没有内容"的意思，造型圆润、配色温和，不会让人觉得冷冰冰。

用的时候要注意两点：
- 它只有 320×246，放大会发糊，建议在空状态区域里按 160px 左右的宽度显示，或者找一张更大的版本。
- 奶油色和深色背景搭配时，机身可能会和背景糊在一起。如果你的产品有深色模式，最好单独测一下，或者给它配一个浅色底板。
```

Those wait for the user's Go. Do not change unrelated tests. If the root cause sits in production code that needs a design decision, stop and report it instead of guessing.
